class="coh-heading text-with-image__heading"> Reduce Check Fraud with Positive Pay   </h2>   <div class="coh-wysiwyg text-with-image__body p2-reg coh-ce-c6f467b4-84904d59">    <p>Match issued checks against those presented for payment to stop fraudulent activity before it happens.&nbsp;</p><p>Our automated Positive Pay<sup>1</sup> solution helps protect your business from check alteration and unauthorized withdrawals.</p><ul><li>Automatically detects mismatches and exceptions</li><li>Customizable review and approval workflows</li><li>Simple integration with your current treasury tools</li></ul> </div>       <div class="coh-container text-with-image__buttons coh-ce-c6f467b4-a586471a">       <a href="/commercial/treasury-management/positive-pay" class="coh-link button  <div class="coh-container text-with-image__content">       <span class="coh-inline-element text-with-image__preheading preheading coh-ce-c6f467b4-a586471a">Our Featured Client</span>       <h2 class="coh-heading text-with-image__heading"> Plaza Hotel &amp; Casino </h2>   <div class="coh-wysiwyg text-with-image__body p2-reg coh-ce-c6f467b4-84904d59">    <p>The Plaza’s 1 Main Street address is literally the birthplace of Las Vegas. Originally the site of the Union Pacific train depot, in 1971 it became home to the Union Plaza, which was the world’s largest hotel-casino at the time. Later renamed to the Plaza Hotel &amp; Casino, today it remains an eye-catching property under the leadership of CEO Jonathan Jossel. Western Alliance Bank has been part of their story since 2010, bringing a responsive, relationship-driven approach that has supported the Plaza’s bold ideas with reliable execution.</p><p>“I’ve been banking with Western Alliance since 2010, and with everyone I’ve worked with, the relationship’s always been incredible. No matter what we’ve needed or what part of the business it’s related to, we’ve always had great service.” Jonathan Jossel CEO Plaza Hotel &amp; Casino</p> </div>       <div class="coh-container class="coh-heading text-with-image__heading"> Neighborhood House Association </h2>   <div class="coh-wysiwyg text-with-image__body p2-reg coh-ce-c6f467b4-84904d59">    <p>Nestled in the heart of San Diego, the Neighborhood House Association (NHA) stands as a pillar of community support, evolving to meet the shifting needs of the community and impacting more than 3.5 million people over the last 50 years alone.</p><p>"Western Alliance Bank maintains that community common touch, even though they're a major financial institution. This is why we can sit down and have a meeting of the minds, because they understand our needs." ~ Rudy Johnson, President and CEO, Neighborhood House Association</p> </div>       <div
